- T-win-50043 [S] smoke/engines embed-JIT swaps zero functions on win-PE | DONE 2026-08-21T16:40Z | SHA c63523bd | REF DETAILS.md#t-win-50043-embed-jit-no-swap-resolved-2026-08-21 -- NOT an embed-JIT bug: the win cmake-release is MCC_CONFIG_JIT=OFF (MCC_JIT_DEFAULT=0), so programs bake def_on=0 into the boot-swap gate (`__on = getenv("MCC_JIT")?…:def_on`) and don't JIT by default -> the jit-parity subject runs pure-AOT -> the arm sees no swap. The ENGINE works (trivial int f(int) + the subject both bake+swap with MCC_JIT=1; lin confirmed Linux swaps 7x via boot-sync route=kgc). FIX (tools/smokerun.c): force MCC_JIT=1 in the jit arm (overrides def_on; the arm must force JIT, not depend on the build default — its purpose is to catch an AOT-in-jit-clothing run). Fixes smoke/engines+/-identity/-known-positive on the untouched OFF build; no-op on JIT-default-on builds (Linux). Diagnostic chain corrected two of my own wrong reads (an empty-stderr PowerShell redirect artifact, and a "no hot+eligible fn" hypothesis — the swaps are boot-sync not hot-triggered). Rejected alt: flip win MCC_CONFIG_JIT=ON (validated exec 8254/8255 green but regresses slice/cref-oracle via per-program JIT-boot overhead). Deferred policy Q: should win adopt repo-default MCC_CONFIG_JIT=ON (needs a game-off window to re-validate the slice test)?

- T-lin-10525 [S] coop M:N fiber-park deadlock (>nproc blocked fibers) | DONE 2026-08-22T01:39Z | SHA f747f581 | REF DETAILS.md#t-lin-10525-coop-mn-fiber-park -- M:N backend used real pthread mutex/cond for user mtx_t/cnd_t, so a fiber blocking in cnd_wait sank its pthread WORKER into a kernel wait; with >nproc simultaneously-blocked fibers every worker was pinned and the pipeline deadlocked (coro-prime-sieve hung N>=~40 under mcc-coop-mn; fine on the 8 other configs). FIX: cooperative M:N -- cooperative mtx_t/cnd_t (no kernel-blocking user wait), lock-handoff protocol (scheduler lock held across each fiber<->scheduler ctx-swap, so the single pthread mutex is only locked/unlocked by one thread even as fibers migrate), __mcc_mn_block parks the FIBER and returns the worker to the run loop, cnd_wait releases+parks atomically (no lost wakeup), external threads (main) block via a shadow fiber on __mcc_mn_extern, errno saved/restored across parks (per-fiber TLS survives migration), token-based thrd_join + scheduler-reaped detached fibers. Dropped the coro_prime_sieve x mcc-coop-mn SKIP in run.sh. VERIFY: coro completes N=20..2000 (17389@2000) == mcc-coop == mcc-nat == gcc == clang; c11_threads_coop_mn + _tls green; non-MN coop/mt/single unchanged; 100/100 TLS + 30/30 deep-pipeline (200 fibers >> 32 workers) stress clean -O0..-O4; full tests/benchmarks green (588/588, mcc==gcc==clang). Residual perf follow-up (NOT a bug): O(N) __mcc_all wake scan makes deep coop/coop-mn pipelines O(N^2)-slow at large N.

- T-lin-10526 [S] runtime GPU compute offload + mandelbrot CPU/multi-GPU hybrid (P3 codegen oracle) | DONE 2026-08-22T03:12Z | SHA c1037393 | REF DETAILS.md#t-lin-10526-gpu-hybrid-oracle -- new runtime/include/mcc_gpu_offload.h: reusable Vulkan compute offload a COMPILED mcc program links (-lvulkan) to run a data-parallel loop across GPU(s)+CPU; multi-device enum (mcc_gpu_num_devices/open_nth, score-ranked, shaderFloat64-required, MCC_GPU_DEVICE override), host-visible SSBO, push-constant dispatch, no-abort CPU fallback. mandelbrot.c -DMCC_GPU_OFFLOAD offloads via a DYNAMIC WORK-STEALING QUEUE (shared atomic row cursor; small CPU chunks/core, larger GPU chunks) -> self-balances heterogeneity + non-uniform per-row cost, no calibration. KEY FINDING: fp64 (needed for CPU-exact parity) is crippled on consumer GPUs, so the 32-thread CPU is fastest and RTX beats Radeon only ~1.8x (not 10x); the scheduler still splits proportionally.
